- **OctD4.1-R16** [Oct D4.1 @ 07:36] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.1 Go over ngày 3/s_00441.jpg. Psychology is not the root problem; if you know in advance a spot is a no-trade, you simply do not trade there. VN: "Cái vấn đề nó không phải nằm ở tâm lý nữa ... Bây giờ nếu mà tui biết ngay chỗ đó tui không trade được"
- **OctD4.1-R17** [Oct D4.1 @ 07:43] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.1 Go over ngày 3/s_00461.jpg. Trade only when the conditions you set in advance ('lực') are all met; if not, do nothing. That is why 3 losses in a row did not break him. VN: "khi nào nó đáp ứng đủ điều kiện những lực mà mình đặt ra thì tui mới trade, còn không, tui không làm gì hết"
- **OctD4.1-R18** [Oct D4.1 @ 08:09] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.1 Go over ngày 3/s_00470.jpg. The cause of blow-ups is sizing too large. VN: "banh nặng. Lý do? Đánh sizing quá lớn"
- **OctD4.1-R20** [Oct D4.1 @ 09:32] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.1 Go over ngày 3/s_00570.jpg. A funded account bought for about $130 does not give a usable $50,000: you have only about $2,000 of room (heard 'suplot', likely a drawdown/loss limit; platform heard 'lưu xích', likely Lucid - unsure). VN: "Anh chị bỏ 130 đô ra để anh chị chỉ có 2.000 đô suplot. Chưa bao giờ mọi người có được 50.000 đô suplot"
- **OctD4.1-R21** [Oct D4.1 @ 09:56] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.1 Go over ngày 3/s_00590.jpg. Sizing arithmetic: 3 morning trades of about 20 points each = 60 points. 60 points on 1 NQ is a ~$1,200 loss ('thua ngàn 2'); on 2 NQ the account is blown. Those who traded 5-6 'enkyu' (read MNQ) were not blown. VN: "3 lệnh hồi sáng mỗi lệnh 20 điểm là 60 điểm. Nếu mà anh chị đánh 1 enkyu là thua ngàn 2, 2 enkyu là banh nặng ... anh chị đánh 5 enkyu, 6 enkyu hồi s
- **OctD4.1-R24** [Oct D4.1 @ 11:45] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.1 Go over ngày 3/s_00701.jpg. If you have drawn the box, you know the market is faking out, and you still enter knowing you will lose, that loss is your own fault. VN: "khi anh chị vẽ ra được cái box rồi mà anh chị vẫn cố tình anh chị vô lệnh thì cái đó là lỗi do ai? ... tui biết khúc này sai quay ... tui vô tui sẽ t
- **OctD4.1-R25** [Oct D4.1 @ 12:52] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.1 Go over ngày 3/s_00761.jpg. With only 30 minutes a day (08:30-09:00 CT open) you are not forced to trade fake-outs: if it does not run today it will run tomorrow; the market is always there. VN: "chỉ có 30 phút buổi sáng open từ 8h30 đến 9h thôi ... market nó lúc nào nó cũng còn đó ... Hôm nay nó không chạy, ngày mai nó sẽ chạy mà"
- **OctD4.1-R26** [Oct D4.1 @ 13:28] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.1 Go over ngày 3/s_00801.jpg. If you have drawn the box and know it is a fake-out day, switch the machine off. VN: "Anh chị đã dễ [vẽ] được cái box là cái thứ nhất. Anh chị biết nó sai quay. Thì làm ơn cứ việc tắt máy"
- **OctD4.1-R27** [Oct D4.1 @ 14:05] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.1 Go over ngày 3/s_00841.jpg. Why: a blow-up forces you to redo everything (about 2 days pass + 5 days build buffer = about 7 days), while 3 losses alone cost little; a $150 winning day the next day still keeps payout rights. VN: "anh chị phải làm lại ... 2 ngày đánh pass, rồi thêm 5 ngày ... build buffer nữa, là 7 ngày ... Nếu ngày mai anh chị đánh lại 150 anh chị vẫn có quyền
- **OctD4.1-R29** [Oct D4.1 @ 15:40] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.1 Go over ngày 3/s_00921.jpg. If touching the market will blow you up, go do something else to forget it; life is not only trading (family, work, self). VN: "nếu như tôi đụng vô, tôi sẽ banh nặt, thì bây giờ tôi đi kiếm cái việc gì đó tôi làm để tôi quên cái chuyện đó đi"
- **OctD4.1-R31** [Oct D4.1 @ 17:20] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.1 Go over ngày 3/s_01038.jpg. Do not force a trade when you know it will hurt; ramming into it only hurts more and gains nothing. VN: "biết sẽ đau rồi đừng có cố gắng đâm đầu vô nó càng đau thêm chứ chả có được cái gì hết"
- **OctD4.2-R18** [Oct D4.2 @ 14:18] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.2 Setup gồng lời và cách move stoploss/s_00857.jpg. Runner size: take the partial at +20 points and leave just 1 MNQ running; 1 MNQ riding about 100 points is worth +$200 (MNQ $2 per point). VN: "mà anh chị bán hold ở 20 điểm mà anh chỉ chừa 1 M&Q [MNQ] | mà anh chỉ 1 M&Q mà ăn 100 điểm là cũng được thêm 200 đô cho 1 M&Q"
- **OctD4.2-R21** [Oct D4.2 @ 17:45] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.2 Setup gồng lời và cách move stoploss/s_01069.jpg. Do not average up / add on this setup; nobody can be 100% sure it runs, so you take a partial instead (answer to a student: why sell part instead of buying more). VN: "Cam đoan chắc chắn với em là 100% nó chạy không? Không bao giờ. Còn anh chị nào dám up? | Mình không biết được 100% nó có chạy hay không"
- **OctD4.2-R22** [Oct D4.2 @ 18:35] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.2 Setup gồng lời và cách move stoploss/s_01109.jpg. Managing risk matters more than being a good trader (said since day 1). VN: "Em nói mọi người là cái việc mà mình trade có giỏi hay không đó, nó không quan trọng bằng cái việc mình manage cái risk."
- **OctD4.3-R23** [Oct D4.3 @ 17:25] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.3 Multi Time Frame/s_01045.jpg. Goal: raise the win rate of reversal trades with rules, not by shorting just because price touched resistance; trade reversals 'with knowledge', not 'jungle' style. VN: "mình phải làm sao mình tăng cái win rate ... chứ không thể nào mà mình đánh đảo chiều mình đụng resistance cái mình đánh xuống"
- **OctD4.3-R73** [Oct D4.3 @ 1:12:00] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.3 Multi Time Frame/s_04319.jpg. Anyone who wants to trade comfortably should NOT attempt these mini-level scalps; they are tiny levels, 'guerrilla' style needing courage (he nicknames it 'dựa cua hồn', ASR unsure). VN: "Còn những anh chị nào mà muốn trade một cách thoải mái thì đừng, đừng cố gắng như thế này ... du kích ... Lớp mình thì gọi là dựa cua hồn"
- **OctD4.3-R79** [Oct D4.3 @ 1:18:20] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.3 Multi Time Frame/s_04703.jpg. Learning time differs per person (1 month, 2 months, up to 6 months); the way is recap + practice, no fixed duration. VN: "mỗi người nó sẽ có cái time clock khác nhau ... Sẽ có người là 1 tháng được, có người 2 tháng được có người 6 tháng, nhưng anh chị phải recap và anh 
- **OctD4.3-R80** [Oct D4.3 @ 1:18:54] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.3 Multi Time Frame/s_04743.jpg. Learning exercise: buy the cheapest test account (a ~$10-20 Bulenox test), enter 1 MNQ and then sit and watch it run for a week; after entering, stay in front of the screen and do nothing else (no mouse). VN: "anh chị mua 1 cái attest. Vô 1 MNQ, rồi ngồi ngắm nó chạy cho tới cuối giờ ... đúng 1 tuần lễ ... Mua 1 account Bulenox ... mười mấy hai chục đồng ..
- **OctD4.3-R81** [Oct D4.3 @ 1:20:51] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 4.3 Multi Time Frame/s_04863.jpg. During the watch exercise he describes not moving the stop to break-even and waiting; admits the emotional cost (price breaks resistance, waiting hours) and in hindsight 'kéo break-even / lấy 5-10 điểm'. This is a beginner-experience narrat VN: "Nó rớt xuống lại ... mình có nên kéo về break even không? Không, để đó đi ... Biết như vậy mới nãy về break even, kéo break even đi hoặc lời 5 điểm, 
- **OctD5.1-R14** [Oct D5.1 @ 03:52] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.1 Go over ngày 4/s_00229.jpg. When candles run a lot you get FOMO and enter randomly; remember this. VN: "Cái mà mọi người hay quên là khi anh chị thấy nến nó chạy nhiều quá, anh chị là bị phô mô [FOMO] và anh chị cứ vô lệnh tầm bậy tầm bạ."
- **OctD5.1-R15** [Oct D5.1 @ 04:00 (second line) and 04:08] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.1 Go over ngày 4/s_00237.jpg. If you do not enter at the right place you are already ~70% wrong; the remaining ~30% depends on luck. VN: "khi mà anh chị không vô đúng cái chỗ mà anh chị cần vô thì là mình đã sai 70% rồi và còn 30% còn lại mình đang dựa vào may mắn"
- **OctD5.1-R16** [Oct D5.1 @ 04:15] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.1 Go over ngày 4/s_00237.jpg. Trading is right or wrong; there is no luck. (If luck mattered anyone would take payouts.) VN: "trade 1 là đúng 2 là sai chứ không có may mắn. Nếu mà trade có sự may mắn thì thiệt sự là ai cũng trade được và ai cũng có thể lãnh lương rồi."
- **OctD5.1-R25** [Oct D5.1 @ 07:46-07:58] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.1 Go over ngày 4/s_00465.jpg. Trading against the move (seeing it fall and pressing long/buy against it, stubbornly) is your own fault, not the system's. VN: "market này đánh Insepa only thôi là cũng thắng, chỉ khi nào mà anh chị cố chấp… thấy nó rớt, bấm nút long, bấm nút bai, bấm ngược lên, thì cái lúc đó
- **OctD5.1-R26** [Oct D5.1 @ 08:16-08:26] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.1 Go over ngày 4/s_00492.jpg. Follow the rules we set for the market, not press whatever you feel like. VN: "Cái việc của mọi người là mọi người cố gắng [follow/update] những điều luật của mình đặt ra cho market, chứ không phải là thích thế nào thì bấm thế đ
- **OctD5.1-R27** [Oct D5.1 @ 08:26-08:39] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.1 Go over ngày 4/s_00506.jpg. Put yourself into a framework (khuôn khổ); neither he nor any other instructor can help if you press whatever way you like. VN: "nếu mà mọi người thích thế nào thì bấm thế đó… những người đứng lớp khác cũng không thể nào giúp anh chị được, anh chị phải đưa bản thân mình vô một 
- **OctD5.1-R28** [Oct D5.1 @ 08:40-08:58] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.1 Go over ngày 4/s_00526.jpg. Trade the same single operation every day, like a robot: the same setup, every single day. Trading is boring and hard. VN: "Tray nó là cái công việc rất nhàm, nhưng cũng rất khó… anh chỉ tray một kiểu, anh chỉ làm một thao tác every single day. Và anh chỉ làm nó như là một
- **OctD5.1-R30** [Oct D5.1 @ 09:47] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.1 Go over ngày 4/s_00586.jpg. Nobody should try to become a full-time trader; trading is a very boring job. VN: "Cái nghề trade là cái nghề rất nhàm chán và em nói mọi người đừng ai đừng anh chị nào cố gắng để làm full time em nói thiệt."
- **OctD5.1-R31** [Oct D5.1 @ 10:27-10:36] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.1 Go over ngày 4/s_00626.jpg. Thinking "I must go full time" becomes pressure and a psychological barrier; just earn extra. VN: "thì nó sẽ làm cái áp lực, một cái rào cản tâm lý cho mọi người, nên đừng ai suy nghĩ chuyện full time, cứ bình thường, mình cứ kiếm extra"
- **OctD5.1-R32** [Oct D5.1 @ 10:37-10:45] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.1 Go over ngày 4/s_00626.jpg. Grow income in steps: extra ~500-1,000/month, then 1,000-2,000, then 2,000-3,000 (as you become consistent); extra 5-10 thousand is the far-future goal; near goal is a small extra.
- **OctD5.1-R33** [Oct D5.1 @ 10:48-11:09] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.1 Go over ngày 4/s_00646.jpg. Do not try to jump to tens of thousands a month in one go; nobody does it in one month, and beginners who do "do not last long". VN: "đừng ai muốn nhảy lên 1 cái là đùng… kiếm mấy chục ngàn tháng… Những ai mà mới vừa trade mà kiếm được mấy chục ngàn tháng, em nói những người đó khôn
- **OctD5.1-R34** [Oct D5.1 @ 11:16-11:31] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.1 Go over ngày 4/s_00674.jpg. Beginner's luck: earning too much at the very start makes you cocky ("ý y", unsure) — so earn slowly. VN: "khi mà cái thời điểm đầu tiên mọi người biết begin to luck [beginner's luck] mà đúng không? Thời điểm đầu tiên mà mọi người kiếm được quá nhiều tiền 
- **OctD5.1-R35** [Oct D5.1 @ 11:57-12:20] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.1 Go over ngày 4/s_00714.jpg. Don't compare yourself to people earning tens of thousands a month and don't be greedy; each person has a different timeline ("time lock", unsure); keep building a system and it improves. VN: "Đừng bao giờ suy nghĩ là anh này, anh nhìn anh này, anh kia kiếm mấy chục ngàn tháng, đừng ham. Tại vì mỗi người sẽ có một cái time lock khác nhau."
- **OctD5.1-R36** [Oct D5.1 @ 12:55] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.1 Go over ngày 4/s_00775.jpg. Do not quit your main job; keep working until you have 2-3 income streams. VN: "Đừng nghỉ chấp chính [job chính], khuyên thiệt… cứ trade đi, nhưng vẫn phải đi làm cho tới khi mọi người có từ 2 tới 3 [nguồn] income."
- **OctD5.1-R37** [Oct D5.1 @ 14:40-15:20] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.1 Go over ngày 4/s_00874.jpg. If you have no monthly income, keep going to work (maybe fewer hours): money in and out reduces pressure; staying at home without income/trading profit is more psychological pressure. VN: "nếu mọi người không có nguồn income nào vô mỗi tháng thì đừng nghỉ… cứ việc đi làm, có thể là đi làm ít lại nhưng vẫn phải đi làm để có đồng ra đồng 
- **OctD5.1-R38** [Oct D5.1 @ 17:53-18:10] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.1 Go over ngày 4/s_01060.jpg. Keep trying and doing recaps consistently and you will reap results; only random pressing of buy/sell (not knowing what you are doing) fails. VN: "Anh Eric hồi xưa, anh cũng làm recap liên tục, liên tục… Lúc cứ cố gắng là sẽ gặt trái ngọt thôi. Chỉ khi nào mà anh chị không cố gắng anh chị cứ bấm
- **OctD5.2-R30** [Oct D5.2 @ 16:56] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.2 Cách vẽ và trade tại major levels/s_01003.jpg. — If the stop from the first-candle entry is a bit big, reduce size (rather than moving to a closer candle). -
- **OctD5.3-R19** [Oct D5.3 @ 08:09] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.3 Cách kết hợp và trade tại indicators/s_00489.jpg. Entering while price is only near the line (before it touches) gets you swept: example on screen, price came back near the EMA, did not touch, then the touch candle came and price fell ~95 points. VN: "Nó về gần tới nè nó chưa đụng nè. Đó anh chị nào vô đi ... Rồi xíu nó quét"
- **OctD5.3-R20** [Oct D5.3 @ 08:18] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.3 Cách kết hợp và trade tại indicators/s_00497.jpg. On a big-candle day the pre-touch entries still survive and even profit; on a small-candle day the same entries get wrecked ('banh xác', 'chọt lũng bụng') - so wait for the touch regardless. VN: "Tại vì hôm nay nến nó khá là bự nên là mọi người thấy mọi người vẫn có ăn đó. Nhưng mà nếu mà thật sự mà nến nhỏ ... là banh xác rồi, chọt lũng bụng"
- **OctD5.3-R21** [Oct D5.3 @ 08:29] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.3 Cách kết hợp và trade tại indicators/s_00509.jpg. Waiting for the candle to touch keeps your risk very low; skipping the touch 'stabs you in the belly'. VN: "đợi nến đụng giúp em thì mình cái khả năng cái risk của mình nó rất là thấp, còn không là nó chọt lũng bụng đó"
- **OctD5.3-R29** [Oct D5.3 @ 12:09] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.3 Cách kết hợp và trade tại indicators/s_00729.jpg. A student's problem (getting filled on the running candle) is an execution/operation problem, not a candle problem; you may simply wait for the candle to close and then place the order. VN: "vấn đề này là vấn đề thao tác chứ không phải vấn đề nến ... Bây giờ chị có thể đợi nến nó đóng luôn, chị quăng vô cũng được"
- **OctD5.3-R30** [Oct D5.3 @ 12:30] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.3 Cách kết hợp và trade tại indicators/s_00749.jpg. If you stage the order before the candle closes, keep it about 5-6 points away from the running candle price and let it run. VN: "Thì để cách tầm 5-6 điểm đi rồi để nó chạy đi"
- **OctD5.3-R31** [Oct D5.3 @ 12:33] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.3 Cách kết hợp và trade tại indicators/s_00749.jpg. Submit the order when about 1 second is left on the candle; keep the mouse/click ready and send it the moment the candle closes ('information problem, not a candle problem'). VN: "Chị nào còn 1 giây thì quăng vô ... Cứ để xa ra, cứ cầm sẵn đi, click chuột để sẵn đi. Khi nào thấy nó đóng quăng ngược lên"
- **OctD5.3-R33** [Oct D5.3 @ 13:40] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.3 Cách kết hợp và trade tại indicators/s_00819.jpg. Common mistake: after the candle closes above EMA 34 and holds, do NOT expect price to run straight to the fish; it never runs in one go, and may never reach the fish at all. VN: "khi nến đóng trên e34 rồi ... anh chị muốn đánh lên con cá, thường anh chị expect nó sẽ phải exactly chạy về con cá ... không bao giờ nó chạy một phá
- **OctD5.3-R34** [Oct D5.3 @ 14:20] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.3 Cách kết hợp và trade tại indicators/s_00856.jpg. Do not carry the mindset 'it holds EMA 34, therefore it will go to the fish'. VN: "làm ơn đừng có tư tưởng là nó hold được EMA 34 là nó sẽ về cá, nha. Làm ơn giùm em"
- **OctD5.3-R45** [Oct D5.3 @ 21:09] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.3 Cách kết hợp và trade tại indicators/s_01269.jpg. Calibration of the claim: the with-trend EMA 9 trades 'ăn rất nhiều' - he immediately corrects himself: not 'win a lot' but 'they go fairly OK'. VN: "Những cái lệnh này là những lệnh ăn rất là nhiều. Không phải là ăn rất là nhiều mà thật sự là nó sẽ đi khá là ok"
- **OctD5.4-R11** [Oct D5.4 @ 04:47] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.4 5_ trigger nhưng 1_ trade ngược chiều/s_00285.jpg. Counter-5m orders: reduce the size. VN: "những cái lệnh đó, anh chị giảm size lại"
- **OctD5.4-R20** [Oct D5.4 @ 07:51-08:20] /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/scenes/Ngày 5.4 5_ trigger nhưng 1_ trade ngược chiều/s_00460.jpg. You must know you are in a conflict: if the big frame says short and you buy, those buys must be scalps, small size, take profit as soon as there are points. VN: "Nhưng mình conflict, mình sẽ phải hiểu mình đang làm cái gì. Là nếu như mà mình đánh ngược khung giờ lớn... những lệnh long của mình phải là những lệ

## 7. Disputed and unresolvable: rulings and questions for the mentor

### 7a. Rulings on the 53 rules that failed verification in pass 1

A third party re-read audio (two Whisper models plus first pass) and opened the frames. Result: 43 corrected, 6 confirmed as written, 1 rejected, 3 unresolvable (ask the mentor). The corrected text below REPLACES the pass-1 wording. Where only the time cite moved, the rule stands and the new time is given.

Corrected or confirmed, by video:

**D1.1**
- R12 corrected: Old Apex: trade up to 2,600 to be allowed to withdraw 2,000; 100 per day (20 days = 2,000, plus 6 days = 2,600); size "1 hay 2 man kill" (garbled, micro NOT established, unsure); about day 22 a very bad day, got angry ("nổi máu lên, em tăng size"), raised size, blew everything built over about 20 days. [Oct D1.1 @ 10:00-10:48]. Do not encode "micros".
- R29 corrected: the newcomer's current target is wrong; an extra ~1,000 a month already puts you ahead of others [29:20-29:45] ("extra thôi một ngàn đô... là anh chị đã hơn người khác rồi"), not 40-50k a month. "Extra 1,000-2,000 a month is more than enough" is at 32:45 (also 30:51).
- R59 corrected: mistake 5 is announced at 58:14-58:38 ("cái điều mà mọi người phải tự nhìn lại", after "em mới kể 4 điều") and is NOT cut off: after "Nói sao ta?" he says you do not accept the loss and are not serious enough to understand why you lost; no successful trader skips the recap (59:00-59:14). [B] mistake 5 = not recapping / not understanding why you lost. Conflict: D1.1-R24 gives a different "mistake 5" (no plan, 17:48-18:26). Two readings in the same video, unresolved.

**D1.2**
- R52 corrected: never increase size; he trades the same size as the students and does not re-enter to win back a loss ("em không bao giờ em tăng size", "thua em không thèm vô gỡ luôn", [35:14-35:37]). The "3 MNQ" count is unsure (large-v3 only).

**D2.1**
- R3 corrected: print slide 16 (the five market rules) about TEN copies ("tầm một chục tấm", said at 03:06 and 03:17), on A4, and stick them wherever your eyes land: desk, in front of you while trading, beside the bed, fridge, restroom. Not 12, not 10-12. Frame s_00197 is slide 16 "Rules và sự thật về market".

**D2.2**
- R19 confirmed as written; the cite is 11:20-13:31 (student's wick answer rejected 12:02; body-only explanation 12:29-12:39; "wick, ignore it" 13:29-13:31).
- R30 corrected: SHORT stop loss at the TOP of the inside bar because you enter at its bottom (reverse for Long). Full quote 26:40; short form 21:20.
- R42 corrected: the cancel rule is mandatory for today only [35:56-36:00]; the cases where an order may be kept are taught tomorrow, said at [1:04:00].
- R75 corrected: never judge a setup pretty or ugly from outside; place the order, enter, manage; he never calls a trade pretty on livestream. [1:14:54-1:15:00].

**D2.3**
- R6 corrected: failure of the old-top Buy Stop: fills on a small push (~30,760), reverses, sweeps the stop; repeated about a week and blew accounts: "my thinking was wrong from the beginning". [03:47-04:25]; the sketch low is near 30,330-30,340, not 30,380.
- R15 corrected: read the trend with the naked eye, left to right, no analysis: here it is going up. Spoken 13:00-13:20 (frame s_00791 at 13:11), not 12:11.
- R21 corrected: price follows the direction of the break: break of the previous HIGH goes up, break of the previous LOW goes down. Quote at 19:32. The fill is the Buy Stop 29,644.75; 29,658.00 is a bar open.
- R23 REJECTED: he places the Buy Stop once he sees 3 red candles (1-2-3), not at the 2nd. The 3-red minimum is at 18:27-18:39 (two models); the "2" at 18:43 is a single-model garble. Frame s_01124 shows 3 reds.
- R25 corrected: place the Buy Stop on top of the red candle (on screen high + 0.50; "slightly above" is not said) and drag it to each newly closed red candle's high until a candle exceeds the previous high. It fills at the Buy Stop (21:00-21:31). Prices from s_01272, s_01277, s_01291; 29,658 is the open of the breakout bar.
- R26 corrected: a setup must supply three things: entry, stop loss, target. The sentence is at 26:39-26:46. At 23:11 he only asks what is missing and the room answers Stop Loss and Target.
- R31 corrected: with the stop at the candle bottom and the target near the old high, R:R rises to about 1:2 ("no longer 1:1"). Screen: 2.11, or 1.83 in a later frame (s_01655, s_01660, s_01705).
- R46 corrected: buffer (trừ hao): entry 1 pt beyond the candle low and stop 1 pt beyond the candle high = 2 pts total. "Never forget it; it saves you on choppy days." The spoken digits DO match the chart with the leading 28 dropped (s_02872: L 28,317.25, H 28,330.75). Delete the pass-1 "digits do not match" clause.
- R62 corrected: price often bounces before reaching the old low, so target near it, not at it (1:10:00, s_04200). Separately, the trailed Sell Stop ORDER must be far enough from price to manage (1:10:26-1:10:47). Delete "a too-close target gets swept".
- R63 corrected: a far target takes longer, but only some minutes, "not that long"; the "4-5 minutes" is heard by one model only (unsure).

**D3.2**

- R38 confirmed: draw a line only to trade it; do not draw and sit watching. Verbatim 34:00; paraphrase 25:48.
- R45 corrected: a nearby low (the second dip after the opening spike, about 17:29-17:31) also counts as a bottom on the line; the "not the first" clause is at 33:32, not 31:20.
- R59 corrected: a trendline needs two bottoms first, then price returns to it (41:20). The 18:17 / 28,645.50 value is the placed drag handle, not a bottom; the two bottoms are about 17:04-17:08 and 17:30.
- R63 UNRESOLVABLE, ask the mentor (question 5).

He speaks this as a hypothetical (55:11-55:29) and never calls the 21:16 candle an inside bar (its high exceeds the prior high; 28,775.75 is the stop tag).

- R32 confirmed: colour the FTGH box RED (default white) because it is resistance (22:40, 23:14, 23:32; s_01367 picker, s_01418 red box). The "20% fill" is Opacity 20% in the picker.
- R37 corrected: a short at a location needs two confirmations: (1) price touches the level, (2) a candle closes below it (rejection, since it is resistance); then place the sell stop. 31:56-32:16, again 29:44 and 30:01-30:06.
- R56 corrected: NOT a trading rule. A demo remark: he deletes an FTGL box he finds too big and picks a smaller example "cho mọi người dễ được" (44:10-44:19). No size filter is taught.

**D4.1**
- R23 corrected: when the morning box is drawn (08:30 session did not move for about 30 minutes), colour it red and do not touch the area; trade only after price exits (11:00-11:40). Left edge about 08:33-08:34 (s_00698, s_00998); box prices in s_00701.
- R30 corrected: anecdote, not a rule: in his own evenings his target is small, about 10-20 (unit unsure: points or ticks), then he exits (16:54, first pass only). He is US-based; "Vietnam time" is not in the audio.

**D4.2**
- R14 corrected: on the confluence setup (PHL/PLH plus same-direction inside bar) sell part at +15 or +20 points (your choice), then stop to break-even (plus a small commission cushion), and let it run to the old high; applies within 3-4 candles of entry. Remove the "16-pt stop" and the "1:1" claim: in no audio or frame.
- R29 corrected: the course is updated monthly; follow the most recent months (Aug / Sep / Oct 2026); last year's material (2-minute-candle era, Nov-Dec 2025) stays only as experience because the market can return to those conditions. Drop "Aug/Sep/Oct are older" and "September rules can be superseded". [23:00, 23:54; s_01465 shows folders to 9/2026]

**D4.3**
- R20 corrected: a 1m short is only allowed when the setup/order sits below the 5m sell-trigger line (the 5m confirm). [13:56-14:00]. 28,834.00 is the later redrawn line (s_01364).
- R28 confirmed: above the sell-trigger line no long while the 5m still has a sell trigger; long only after a 5m buy trigger. Answer 23:00-23:17 (question 22:00-22:46).
- R40 confirmed: do not drag the line on every successive buy setup; leave it and move it only on reversal (a sell setup, not a colour change). [35:10-35:55]. The price 29,355.00 appears in s_02112 and s_02404 (same line nudged ~1.5 pt).
- R46 corrected: candle colour is irrelevant; only open vs close matters (open high closing lower = red; open low closing higher = green); read the open point and break/no-break of the previous candle. Quote 42:40, demo 40:40-41:08. Numbers quoted before were live bid/ask.
- R58 corrected: the first 08:30 five-minute candle is not an ISB; the next candle inside it is; box that one. [57:20-57:35]; axis tags 28,727.50 and 28,640.50 (s_03456).
- R59 corrected: 1-3 five-minute candles that have not left the box merge into ONE inside bar; box head to toe; no trade this morning. [58:00-58:20]; end time 11:05; chart is 5m.
- R83 corrected: 1 MNQ, no stop loss (ASR "softbox", unsure), no target, watching only for feel; test account, not PA; 1 pt = $2. [1:23:12-1:24:00]
- R89 corrected: a single FTGH box scares many; experienced students (about 14-15 months, "more than a year") may trade it, new students cannot.

**D5.2**
- R17 UNRESOLVABLE (question 9).
- R20 corrected: after a candle closes below a level and price returns to retest it, the level is now resistance (question 07:42-07:56, answer 13:55-13:58).

**D5.3**
- R43 confirmed: price above fish red, fish white, EMA 34 and EMA 9 = uptrend; it cannot be a downtrend. [20:00-20:13]. "EM14" is an ASR mishear of EMA34.

**D5.4**

- R9 corrected: Scalping Only is a counter-5m order: you enter and, as soon as there are points, you run: pull the stop yourself and sell part off. "có điểm" means once in profit, not "only a few points" ("có điểm là chạy" at 04:25, 05:43, 07:05, 09:38, 11:44). No number is taught.
- R22 corrected: trading against the 5m is something students have never done; they have only just started, so they are not addicted yet; learn, you get used to it slowly. "Do not worry" is not said. The "đăng ký kênh" line at 10:00 is a Whisper invention.
- R24 corrected: example 2: the Buy Setup Trigger is the candle at the yellow line 27,685.75 (same on 1m and 5m). Path: 5m fell from about 27,880 to a low about 27,610, bounced to about 27,740, then a red candle pulled back to the line. 27,740 is the post-low bounce high.
- R28 corrected: in KTAB Bé Na (legend "90 0 20 20") only EMA 34 is checked; EMA5, EMA20, Buy, Sell, BB, VWAP, EMA9 and both Touch options are unchecked (list cut off below). The Bé Na EMA34 swatch is white. The legend also shows "MNQ TS v1.9 15 0 3", "EMA 34 close" (a separate EMA, perhaps the orange line, unsure) and "KTAB Fishtrend +SR" (the white/red stair lines, named). Screen only, no speech.

**D5.5**

- R25 corrected: VEST's only reason against is that the points needed to pass are too LARGE (50-something). On the 15/30/1h/4h EMA lines price sometimes (not often) does not come back, which makes passing hard (10:40-11:14).
- R32 corrected: when account 1 is up about 200 dollars, enter account 2 the opposite way; one side wins, one blows, but you are in the PA (funded account) immediately ("vô luôn PA ngay lập tức" = reaching PA at once, not immediate profit) (13:20, 13:40). Not codeable and not wanted (SIM only).

### 7b. Questions for the mentor (Vietnamese first, English second)

Marked N = new from pass 2.

1. Bán bớt ở 15 điểm khi stop 20 điểm: Oct D1.2 nói "không sao", tháng 9 D1.2 nói không được. Cái nào đúng cho bot? Bán bớt bao nhiêu phần trăm khi bán một phần? (không video nào nói)
   Selling at 15 of a 20-point stop: Oct D1.2 says "no problem", September D1.2 said not allowed. Which governs the bot? What fraction is sold at the partial? (never stated)
2. Trendline: 2 lần chạm là đủ (Oct D3.2, lệnh của thầy) hay phải chạm lần 3 (tháng 9)? Bot đang đợi lần chạm thứ 4. Có phải vào lệnh ở lần quay lại đầu tiên sau 2 điểm?
   Do two touches make a valid line (Oct D3.2) or is the third test required (September)? The bot waits for the 4th touch.
3. Setup gồng lời: cây nến khớp lệnh có tính là cây số 1 không (audio tháng 10: "đếm từ cây nến xanh luôn 1, 2, 3") hay cây sau nó (checklist tháng 9)? Có được cây thứ 4 không? (N: tháng 10 nói "3 tới 4, không có cây thứ 5".)
   Does the fill candle count as #1? Is a 4th spare candle allowed?
4. Break-even: thầy có dời stop qua entry một chút để trừ phí không? Bao nhiêu điểm hoặc tick? (Oct D4.2 chỉ nói 10 lên 11, đơn vị đô.)
   Break-even: do you move the stop a little past entry for fees? How many points or ticks?
5. (N) Ví dụ ngày 1 Nov: nến đỏ 18:40 và nến xanh 18:41. Cây nào là cây "đầu tiên đụng level" để làm chuẩn? Râu chạm line rồi đóng dưới/gần line có tính không? (D3.2-R63: thầy nói "xanh", màn hình cho thấy đỏ chạm trước.)
   In the 1 Nov 18:40 red / 18:41 green example, which candle is the reference "first touching" candle? Does a wick-only touch that closes below or near the line count?
6. Khoảng cách vào lệnh PHL so với đáy cũ: "ít nhất phải cao hơn đáy cũ", "khoảng 15 điểm", hay 3 cây nến? Mức tối thiểu thật là gì? 3 cây chỉ là ước lượng đúng không (Oct D2.3)?
   PHL entry distance from the old low: "at least above the old low", "about 15 points", or 3 candles? What is the real minimum?
7. Thầy trade khung 1 phút cả ngày (Oct D4.2) hay chuyển sang 2 phút sau 09:00 CT? Bot đang chuyển sang 2 phút và EMA 34 cũng đổi theo.
   Do you trade the 1-minute only all day, or switch to the 2-minute after 09:00 CT? The bot switches and the EMA 34 changes with it.
8. Lệnh limit tại EMA 34 khung lớn (Oct D5.5) so với "không bao giờ dùng limit" (tháng 9): limit chỉ dành cho tài khoản VEST thử nghiệm? Bot có bao giờ nên đặt limit không?
   Limit at the big-TF EMA 34 (Oct D5.5) versus "never limit" (September): only for the VEST test account? Should the bot ever place it?
9. (N) D5.2 phút 06:38: câu "cái này cũng được nhưng anh chị không ___ đâu" — chữ bị mất là gì? Swing nào, và có phải "không cần vẽ" không? (Câu kế tiếp lại nói vẽ mọi chỗ đổi màu bình thường.)
   D5.2 @ 06:38: what is the missing word in "this is also OK but you do not ___"? Which swing, and does it mean "no need to draw it"?
10. Level chạm 4 lần thì gãy (tháng 10) hay 5 đến 9 lần (tháng 9)? Cách đếm khác nhau à?
    Does a level that was tested 4 times break (October) or 5 to 9 times (September)?
11. Stop lớn hơn 25-40 điểm: không trade hay trade 1 MNQ? (N: Oct D2.2 nói 12 điểm size 5; 20-30 điểm size 3 hoặc không trade; 40 điểm không trade.) Ranh giới chính xác ở đâu?
    Stop bigger than 25-40 points: no trade, or 1 MNQ? Where exactly is the cut-off?
12. Scalp ngược 5m ở EMA 34 1 phút hoặc support (Oct D5.4) so với "không bao giờ trade ngược trigger 5m" (tháng 9): ngoại lệ tháng 10 có phải luật mới? Stop, size, target của scalp là bao nhiêu? Và 1m short chỉ được dưới đường trigger 5m (D4.3-R20) mâu thuẫn thế nào với scalp?
    Counter-5m scalps (Oct D5.4) versus September "never against the trigger": is the October exception the new rule? Stop, size and target? How does D4.3-R20 fit?
13. (N) "EMA chính" (D5.5 06:40) là EMA 9 hay EMA 34, và chỉ dùng cho khung 1 phút phải không?
    Is the "main EMA" EMA 9 or EMA 34, and is it for the 1-minute chart only?
14. Râu nến xuyên qua có tính là phá không (Oct D5.4, 10:08) cho trendline và level, hay chỉ cho nến trigger?
    Does a wick crossing count as a break (Oct D5.4 @ 10:08) for trendlines and levels, or only trigger candles?
15. Box nến inside 5 phút xóa khi nến 5 phút đóng ngoài (tháng 10) hay nến 1 phút (tháng 9)? Đường trigger có đổi cùng lúc không? (N) Đầu box 28,750 (D4.3-R59) là đỉnh nến mẹ hay đỉnh râu gộp?
    Is the 5m inside-bar box deleted on a 5m close outside (October) or a 1m close (September)? Does the trigger line flip at the same time? Is the box head the mother's high or the union of wicks?
16. Box FTGH/FTGL có hiệu lực bao lâu (sáng hôm sau, 24 giờ, qua cuối tuần)? Khi đỉnh 2 cao hơn đỉnh 1 thì đỉnh box là đỉnh nào?
    How long is an FTGH/FTGL box valid (next morning, 24 hours, over the weekend)? Which peak is the box top when the second is higher?
17. Dừng ngày: sau 3 lệnh thua cộng 1 lệnh thử (tổng 4). Bot mặc định 8. Thầy xác nhận N=4 không?
    Daily stop after 3 losses plus one more try (4). The bot default is 8. Confirm N=4?
18. Bài go-over hằng ngày (tháng 9 D2.1/D3.1) có bỏ trong tháng 10 không? "Không cần theo trend" (Oct D3.1) chỉ dành cho setup retracement?
    Is the daily go-over dropped in October? Is "trend not required" (D3.1) only for retracement setups?
19. Các thuật ngữ chưa định nghĩa: Lo-R2, đường "Fishtrend" (khoảng cách EMA 34 đến cá bao nhiêu là lớn?), nến OB 2 phút, Potential Hello, suplot, "lưu xích".
    Items never defined: Lo-R2, the "Fishtrend" line (how big must the EMA 34 to fish gap be?), the 2-minute outside bar (OB), Potential Hello, suplot, "lưu xích".
20. Vùng supply dưới support và demand trên resistance (slide 17) ngược với quy ước thường. Có chủ ý không?
    Supply under support and demand over resistance (slide 17) is the reverse of the usual convention. Intended?
21. Số tiền chưa khớp: nhu cầu hàng tháng 5-6k (30:26) hay 10k (32:03); lương đầu 7 ngày (tháng 10) hay 3-4 tháng (tháng 9); Apex 20 hay 26 ngày.
    Money figures never reconciled: monthly need 5-6k (30:26) vs 10k (32:03); first payout 7 days (Oct) vs 3-4 months (Sept); Apex 20 vs 26 days.
22. (N) Hôm nay thầy bảo cho học viên vào bao nhiêu MNQ buổi sáng: 3 hay 2? (D1.2-R52, con số 3 chỉ một model nghe.)
    How many MNQ do you tell the students to enter with in the morning, 3 or 2? (D1.2-R52, the "3" is one model only.)
23. (N) Đơn vị mục tiêu buổi tối "10-20" (D4.1-R30) là điểm hay tick trên MNQ?
    Is the evening target "10-20" (D4.1-R30) in points or ticks on MNQ?
24. (N) D4.3: nến 08:30 là nến đầu tiên của phiên, vậy "nến inside nó" tính từ nến 08:25 (Globex) hay không? Bot có nên bỏ qua kiểm tra ISB ở nến 5 phút 08:30?
    Is the first 08:30 candle ever judged against the 08:25 Globex candle? Should the bot skip the ISB check on the 08:30 five-minute bucket?
25. (N) D5.2: sau khi nến đóng dưới level lần đầu trong ngày và giá quay lại test, level đó có thành resistance không nếu trước đó chưa từng giữ được? (Bot chỉ cho phép ISB trong trường hợp này.)
    D5.2: if a level's first touch of the day closes through it and price retests, is it resistance even if it never held earlier that day? (The bot allows only an ISB here.)
26. (N) VEST: nếu tài khoản A chạm stop trước khi đạt +200 đô thì làm gì? Thầy chưa trả lời (D5.5-X3-2).
    VEST: what if account A hits its stop before +$200? Not answered (D5.5-X3-2).
27. (N) Khoảng cách ISB tới level vừa phá: bao nhiêu điểm là "quá gần" (D3.3-R2, R47, R49)? Ví dụ 28,802 trong video khoảng 12-15 điểm.
    How close is "too close" for an ISB to a level it just broke (D3.3-R2, R47, R49)? About 12-15 pts in the 28,802 example.

### 7c. Open items by video

Each video note ends with its own OPEN QUESTIONS / GAPS section: /home/hoang/mm-course/oct/notes/final/<video>.md. Highlights: D2.2 on-screen stop dragged below the mother low (59:13-59:40) while audio says rely on the ISB candle; D3.1 R7 vs R12-R14 (does a break matter by itself?); D4.3 the big frame is 5m "at this time" only; D5.3 EMA 9 needs a touch (Oct) vs loose touch (Sept D5.4-R7); D5.5 limit at EMA 34 with no confirmation vs September.

## Appendix A. Bot vs video: every row where the bot differs or is missing (pass 1, skeptic-corrected)

Pass 2 re-checked these rows. Verdicts did not change except as stated in section 2 (downgraded: LevelEntriesNeedTriggerSide, Scalping Only, second-touch trendline mode). Rows about the PHL buffer (OctD2.3-R46) are superseded by settings table rows 1-2.
Sorted by impact, P1 first (no P0 found). "Bot does" is the evidence from the code read (file:line at commit ca0beb09d, may drift). "Video says" is the Oct rule text. Skeptic corrections are marked. "partial" = half exists.

## Appendix B. Rules found only in pass 2 (61 missed rules)

Found by re-reading each video in extra rounds. 59 were confirmed by two verifiers; 2 are marked UNVERIFIED (OctD2.1-X3-1, OctD5.5-X1-1). "Unsure" marks a single-model or garbled line. Ids start with the video and X = extra. Several are already inside pass-1 rules as only a timeline note; they are listed here because they were not stated as rules.

### D1.1 Psychology
- OctD1.1-X1-1 Size build-up ladder (slide 5): 1) paper trade 3-5 days to get used to the mechanics and the feel of entering orders; 2) 2 MNQ; 3) raise to 3-5 MNQ; 4) raise slowly once confident and familiar with the setups. Raising size suddenly or double-sizing after a loss is a double-edged sword.
- OctD1.1-X1-2 If you do the work (fix the mistakes), in 6 months or 1 year you will look back and thank yourself. He reached consistent income after months of daily struggle, about 2 years ago.
- OctD1.1-X1-3 "Lazy" defined: more free time for yourself, family and kids, not sleeping all day. Sitting at the screen too long is unhealthy, so he walks 1-2 laps around the park. Refines R62.
- OctD1.1-X2-1 Know what you are doing. Do not enter when you feel like it, exit when you feel like it, buy if you like it and sit out if you do not. Trading on whim wastes a lot of time. Follows the instruction to write down the errors you keep making (R19).
- OctD1.1-X3-1 Why a small monthly target is enough: in his nail-salon days (~4,000 a month) he only wished for 1,000 more, because 1,000 spare lets him rest 1-2 days when too tired. Newcomers should copy that modest target, not demand 30-40k a month.
- OctD1.1-X3-2 Seriousness test: if you lose, struggle and still do not post a recap, are you serious about this profession or just leaving it on the shelf? When you are wrong you must find a way to fix it, not shrug "I was wrong right there, I know now". Complements R39.

### D1.2 Risk and reward
- OctD1.2-X1-1 After 3 live losses (300-400 viewers watching) he still takes the 4th trade as normal, with no panic, because he respects the system.
- OctD1.2-X1-2 NEVER average down (DCA). He blew a $200,000 options account at age 21 by averaging down; options plus averaging down cannot survive.
- OctD1.2-X1-3 Trading alone is psychologically relaxed, without 300-400 viewers on your shoulders. After 3 live losses he turns off the live, trades alone and recovers fast.
- OctD1.2-X2-1 A profession is proven by earning something steady from it: an extra $500 or $1,000 a month is enough to show it is a real job, not gambling.
- OctD1.2-X2-2 The more annoyed you are (for example being called a gambler), the more serious you must become.
- OctD1.2-X2-3 (unsure, partly garbled) Now is a golden moment while you can absorb knowledge; once you learn his way you can use it until you stop trading.
- OctD1.2-X2-4 (single model, unsure) Do not let the psychology talk go in one ear and out the other: lie down tonight and ask whether that is you ("100% that is me"). He became calm at 25-26 because the market battered him; he then sat one night, thought, and got serious.
- OctD1.2-X2-5 (single model, unsure) When he loses himself he is not scared; first pass adds that he fears for the students losing and calls that the main point of live trading.
- OctD1.2-X2-6 (single model, unsure) His first live was a losing day and he was sad a whole week; now he is not sad, not from habit or numbness but because he has a system he respects, so when price does not run as hoped he accepts it.
- OctD1.2-X3-1 Casino anecdote: every time he walks into a casino with 500 he leaves with about 5 dollars (the house wins; contrast with having an edge).
- OctD1.2-X3-2 Trading is high pressure: 2-3 years ago he was carefree, now each morning he must force himself up, exhausted; a student told him her body weakens because of the psychology; his most peaceful day is Saturday (no live, market closed).
- OctD1.2-X3-3 (ego wording single model, unsure) Covid-era winning streak: with 1,000 dollars making 200,000 was normal, every pick (penny stocks, AMC, GameStop) won, ego was high; then one day everything blew up and he was too shocked to trade.
- OctD1.2-X3-4 After the blow-up he took a break of about a year or more, not touching market, chart or candles; on return the market was the same; since 2019 (about 7 years) the market keeps running and setups keep coming, so no reason to regret a day not traded.

### D2.1 Support and resistance
- OctD2.1-X1-1 Trading only looks fancy or hard because you have not understood how a candle works. Bad habits come from not understanding the most basic thing. Once you know why the market must move that way, you manage orders better.
- OctD2.1-X1-2 If you apply the five slide-16 rules, you will look at the market with a different eye.
- OctD2.1-X1-3 Over-processing warning: labeling one line "important pivot", another "resistance", another "EMA" and another "mini level" means you process too much. The explanation is promised but not given in this video.
- OctD2.1-X2-1 (low value, partly garbled) A way of trading has a lifespan: some methods work for a stretch (he says about a year, then 6-7 years), then stop working and people hunt for something different. Setup for "the mistake" in R5: do not throw away the whole system; find why you lost and upgrade it.
- OctD2.1-X3-1 UNVERIFIED (meaning partly unsure) Drawing convention: start the resistance line at the first / leftmost test (pre-market about 07:37), not from the nearer right-hand touches.
- OctD2.1-X3-2 The market shows everything; what matters is whether you can see it.

### D2.2 Candle patterns and ISB
- OctD2.2-X1-1 Naming trick: simplify all knowledge into one short phrase; the name tells you what to do. "This is an INSIDE BAR setup" leads straight to placing the order on the inside bar.
- OctD2.2-X1-2 When placing the ISB buy stop / stop loss, do not look at price numbers; read only the candle (buy stop at the ISB top, stop at its bottom).
- OctD2.2-X2-1 Learning order: while you only learn to identify the inside bar, do not think ahead or link it to other questions (when an ISB is a valid profit setup, when not to touch it). Information first, then link. Once identification is understood there are only 2 directions to trade.
- OctD2.2-X3-1 How to tell Ranging from Trending is not taught as a rule yet: theory first, practical chart work soon.

### D2.3 PHL / PLH
- OctD2.3-X1-1 Judge only the nearest move: do not speculate on what comes after (for example that price must make a higher top); here only that a Buy Stop where people sold is the wrong concept.
- OctD2.3-X2-1 Slide 20 "Bear Flag" on screen: price falls, moves sideways (accumulation), then keeps falling; the market is always in 1 of 2 states, Trending or Ranging. Screen only, not spoken.

### D3.1 Go over day 2
- OctD3.1-X1-1 (minor) The short side (downtrend; inside bar Short vs Long) will be covered in more depth today; today's lesson is very confusing for anyone who did not re-watch yesterday's.

### D3.2 Trendline and candle close
- OctD3.2-X1-1 (single model, no candle marked) He points at a red candle (touched and closed below the level) and says it is the resistance; this opens the next block.
- OctD3.2-X1-2 Everything said in class is skill value for the whole trading career; he teaches only condensed material; do not forget it; students who have not memorised it are sent back to study.
- OctD3.2-X2-1 Candle size can never be predicted; if he could know a fall would be that big he would have gone all-in on the single ~1,003-point Friday candle instead of nibbling 15-20 point orders. His own working size is about 15-20 points per order.
- OctD3.2-X2-2 (unsure, single model) There is no extra "Inside Level" setup; a close back above the level alone is not a setup (supports R73 / R79).
- OctD3.2-X2-3 (unsure) Price action before the Sunday gap has no relation to the trendline drawn after it; a gap / new session starts a fresh line.
- OctD3.2-X3-1 A 1m / 2m trendline is not valid tomorrow. How trendlines on the LARGE frames carry across days is deferred to a later lesson.

### D4.3 Multi time frame
- OctD4.3-X1-1 The signal that a 5m uptrend may start to pull back is NOT a support/resistance level and not a trigger. The answer is only a candle breaking the previous candle's low (R7).
- OctD4.3-X1-2 Every up-move has a pullback; price never runs straight in one direction (the exact "2 2 2 low" wording is unsure). That is why the urge to short "right here" appears and why it must be made "educated" by waiting for the 5m trigger (R26).
- OctD4.3-X1-3 Pacing: rewatch the class recordings again and again; concepts are taken in one by one. Before moving on he checks that students understand Bias, Trigger and Setup.
- OctD4.3-X1-4 Do recaps and post them in the group so students correct each other.

### D5.1 Go over day 4
- OctD5.1-X1-1 A student once hoped only to cover grocery money; after building the system she now buys a house. Each person has a different timeline; keeping on improves results (extends R35).
- OctD5.1-X1-2 Why he is full-time while advising others not to be: his work is not only live trading (alerts, lives, testing, helping members, other jobs) and he has 3-4 income streams.
- OctD5.1-X1-3 With trading as extra income keep a reduced part-time job and stay relaxed: work when there are clients, rest when not.
- OctD5.1-X2-1 The reason for "do not enter in the middle of nowhere" is deferred: EMAs and Key Levels are not taught yet.
- OctD5.1-X2-2 (single model, garbled, unsure) If you enter orders properly he guarantees everyone gets paid; the problem only arises when entries are not done properly.
- OctD5.1-X2-3 (single model, garbled, unsure) He himself used to want to earn only a little, not a lot.
- OctD5.1-X3-1 Making money and getting paid from trading is very hard (he knows it). Extends R16.
- OctD5.1-X3-2 The near-term goals (extra 500-1,000 or 1,000-2,000 a month) are PROFIT, and reaching them is already cause to be very happy; 5-10 thousand is only the far goal. Clarifies R32.
- OctD5.1-X3-3 Some other instructors advise quitting the job and trading from home; he says no, keep the main job.
- OctD5.1-X3-4 Course goal: after next month everyone should have a stable trading system so they can get paid (3-4 students got paid that day).

### D5.2 Major levels
- OctD5.2-X1-1 Exam discipline: a quiz answer must be complete and answer only the question asked. For "trade a potential lower high at the resistance level" both checks are needed (touch, then close vs level). Partial answers fail. Trigger-bar and second-leg questions are deferred.
- OctD5.2-X1-2 The second-candle (red) entry is tolerated, but the student owns the risk (higher sweep chance); he cannot watch every student's trades 24/7.

### D5.5 Mindset and VEST
- OctD5.5-X1-1 UNVERIFIED The VEST two-account pass advice is meant for traders in the US. For people in Vietnam he advises against it ("đừng") unless they have strong capital, and says he is doubtful about it.
- OctD5.5-X1-2 Lucid is still fine. Whether to do the VEST plan is the student's own choice with their own money; he only analyses and does not push it.
- OctD5.5-X1-3 The opposite-sides two-account hedge was used at FundedNext before (precedent, not a new rule).
- OctD5.5-X2-1 (unsure, setup name garbled "Nil") Asked whether that setup can be traded on VEST: he says yes ("cũng được") but has not tried it on VEST.
- OctD5.5-X2-2 (unsure) Reason he has not tried it: the VEST instrument does not run properly (choppy single-candle moves); with no buy/sell stop he enters at market when he sees the break (same as R22 / R23).
- OctD5.5-X3-1 (unsure, garbled word "DLL", probably EMA 9) Do not buy at the EMA 9; buy at the normal big-TF EMA 34 level. At the normal level price has to run 300 points before you are blown out, at the EMA 9 a 150 stop is hit at once.
- OctD5.5-X3-2 (unsure) A student asks what happens if the first account blows first in the two-account plan. He does not answer; no rule is given for account A hitting its stop before +$200.
